Sharon AI, Inc. — Sales Intelligence Dossier

Prepared for: Supermicro Sales Team 1 (USA) · Officer US8664 Tuo Cheng · Date: 2026-08-11 Method: Researched through corporate history, financial and registry records, U.S. political leanings, legislative and policy positions, and the company's relationships with its customers. Privately held — evidence comes from state business registries, ARIN/PeeringDB, the operator's own published GPU catalogue and pricing, UCC filings, job postings, community forums and FEC records. Every fact carries its source + date inline. GAP = not found in verified material. No fabrication. Territory: Houston, Texas — Texas Area = T1 | T3. Team 1 can register directly. CRM status: Verified clean 2026-08-11 in salesleads Search (Type = All) — no lead, no account, no do-not-call record. Registrable by Team 1.

FOUR CORRECTIONS TO THE HEADER AND THE WORKING BRIEF — READ BEFORE ANYTHING ELSE

The header above is reproduced verbatim as issued. Four of its premises are contradicted by primary evidence. They are corrected here rather than silently amended, so the working list can be fixed at source.

  1. NOT privately held. The company went public on 2025-12-19 via SPAC merger with Roth CH Acquisition Co. It is SEC-reporting: CIK 0002068385, Commission File 001-43129, Nasdaq: SHAZ (Class A ordinary common stock) and SHAZW (warrants). A Form 10-Q, a 4.6 MB Form S-1, a DEF 14A and multiple 8-Ks exist and were read. The "expect no SEC filings" premise is false, and those filings are by a wide margin the richest evidence in this file (EDGAR submissions API).
  2. NOT Houston. The principal executive office of record is 745 Fifth Avenue, Suite 500, New York, NY 10151. No Houston address appears in any SEC filing, on sharonai.com, or on the careers page. The only US physical presence found anywhere is a small Austin, Texas finance/accounting presence (EDGAR submissions API; Form 8-K 2026-07-24).
  3. The Texas datacenter is gone. Texas Critical Data Centers LLC (TCDC) — the planned 250 MW Ector County / Odessa behind-the-meter build — was sold on 2026-01-13 to JV partner New Era Energy & Digital (NUAI). The 10-Q states the company "ceased to have an ownership interest in TCDC or participate in the joint venture." There is no Texas datacenter play left. This is the single most important correction (Form 10-Q, quarter ended 2026-06-30).
  4. This is not practically a T1 USA hardware opportunity. The 10-Q self-describes the company as "an Australian neocloud operator." Substantially all GPU infrastructure sits with NEXTDC in Melbourne and Sydney, plus GreenSquareDC (Australia) and New Zealand. The buying entity that signs the Lenovo Statement of Work and the executive employment agreements is SharonAI Pty Ltd, an Australian company. Every IXP and facility in the PeeringDB record is Australian, and no ARIN organisation exists for this company at all. The T1 | T3 designation attaches to a Delaware corporate shell with a New York registered office — not to a buying fleet.

Nothing below relies on the corrected premises. Where the brief and the evidence disagree, the evidence is stated and the brief's version is shown as contradicted, not deleted.

1. Bottom line

Sharon AI is a newly public, capital-flooded, Australian-operating neocloud — SharonAI Holdings Inc. (Nasdaq: SHAZ), CIK 0002068385, Delaware-incorporated, New York registered office, trailing half-year revenue of $2,225,396 against roughly $2.27 billion of announced contract value and a ~$4 billion procurement commitment to NVIDIA, run by 70 employees, board members, advisors and contractors (Form 10-Q; Form S-1, 2026-07-31). The honest verdict for this team, stated first because it changes what happens next: this is not a viable Team 1 USA opportunity, and the CRM-clean status is misleading rather than encouraging. The registrable US entity is a holding shell; the buying entity is SharonAI Pty Ltd in Australia; the fleet lives in NEXTDC M3 Melbourne and NEXTDC S3 Sydney plus GreenSquareDC and New Zealand; the only Texas asset — the 250 MW Ector County JV that presumably produced the T1 designation in the first place — was sold on 2026-01-13.

On hardware the picture is equally unambiguous and equally unhelpful. Lenovo is the confirmed incumbent commercial relationship on the compute platform — a 60-month managed-infrastructure Statement of Work dated 2025-12-12 with approximately 50% paid upfront, $33,094 thousand of Equipment-as-a-Service / Device-as-a-Service down payments, an XBRL member tag naming Lenovo Global Financial Services, and the company's own published node specification listing "24 x 32GB TruDDR5 4800MHz RAM", TruDDR5 being Lenovo's proprietary memory brand (10-Q; sharonai.com/ai-cloud/). World Wide Technology (WWT Australia Pty Ltd) is the integrator of record, carrying $256,186 thousand — $256.2 million — of equipment prepayments at 2026-06-30. Cisco is locked in structurally, not merely commercially: Digital Alpha Advisors, which holds an exclusive technology partnership with Cisco, invested up to US$200M and is a shareholder, and the flagship B300 cluster is branded "Australia's first Cisco Secure AI Factory" (S-1; Digital Alpha press release).

Supermicro appears exactly once across the entire evidence set — the 10-Q, the DEF 14A, three 8-Ks and the 4.6 MB S-1 — and it is not as a purchasing relationship. The single reference sits in a list of benefits derived from NVIDIA Cloud Partner status: "OEM Relationships: Facilitated engagement with OEMs such as Cisco, Lenovo, Dell, and Super Micro to support server procurement and integration" (S-1). That establishes membership of an approved OEM panel and nothing more: no purchase order, no prepayment, no commitment, no named agreement, no Supermicro hardware in any published node specification. Grade: circumstantial. Do not tell anyone internally that Supermicro is an incumbent here.

There is one genuine opening, and it is a timing opening rather than a product one. The company consumes hardware as a financed service while carrying $256.2 million prepaid to a single integrator, against ~$765.1 million of near-term purchase commitments, a further ~$217.2 million for 2,048 NVIDIA B300 units, and ~$4 billion contracted with NVIDIA — all on a 70-person team. A brand-new CFO, Anuj Goel, starts 2026-08-24, two weeks after this research date, and inherits that concentration risk. If this account is worked at all, it should be worked in October–November 2026, through the correct geography, on supply-base diversification and delivery certainty for the April–August 2027 five-phase NEXTDC ramp — not on price and not on features. Escalate to APAC/Australia coverage; do not burn one of the five CRM slots on a T1 registration.

1 The group parent is Australian and the US entity is Sharon AI, Inc. CONTRADICTED Inverted. The group parent is SharonAI Holdings Inc., a DELAWARE corporation — CIK 0002068385, Commission File 001-43129, EIN 41-2349750, SIC 7374, principal executive offices 745 Fifth Avenue Suite 500, New York NY 10151, Nasdaq SHAZ with warrants SHAZW on OTC. Former names "Roth CH Holdings, Inc." and "SharonAI Holdings, Inc." The 10-Q states plainly: "SharonAI Inc. ("SA Inc.") is a digital infrastructure provider, incorporated in the state of Delaware in the United States of America on February 15, 2024" — so the entity the brief calls the US entity is itself Delaware, and a wholly owned subsidiary, not a parent. SharonAI Pty Ltd and Distributed Storage Solutions Pty Ltd are operating subsidiaries and subsidiary guarantors. There is no Australian parent, and there is no ASX listing — an ASX secondary listing has been publicly targeted and publicly delayed, and "Sharon AI Ltd" does not exist as a listed entity EDGAR submissions
2 There is no ARIN organisation for Sharon AI, and the company has zero US network presence (the earlier pass of this dossier's own stated negative finding) CONTRADICTED — the single biggest factual error in the prior pass ARIN organisation SOL-111, name "SharonAi", exists and was registered 2024-06-27. It holds AS401247 (ASN name SHARON-AI, registered 2024-07-12) and IPv6 block 2607:C5C0::/32 (NET6-2607-C5C0-1, name SHARON-AI, Direct Allocation, registered 2024-07-17). The identification is not a name coincidence: both ARIN POCs, SNT23-ARIN and SAT42-ARIN, carry companyName "SharonAI PTY LTD" at 303/44 Miller Street, North Sydney NSW 2060 — byte-for-byte the same address as APNIC registrant ORG-SA242-AP. The prior search failed because it queried Sharon* and read the results as unrelated; the matching org is spelled "SharonAi" as one word and was in that very result set. The narrower half of the claim survives: nothing is routed. RIPEstat shows AS401247 announcing no prefixes, and 2607:C5C0::/32 announced=null with 0 of 321 IPv6 RIS peers seeing it. Correct statement: Sharon AI holds dormant, unannounced US number resources at ARIN ARIN org SOL-111

Second: "On June 22, 2026, the Company issued $700 million aggregate principal amount of 4.75% Convertible Senior Notes due June 15, 2032", initial conversion price approximately $99.66. Both indentures independently bar cash dividends without Required Holder consent. 10 The NVIDIA collaboration is real, six-year, and worth about $4 billion CONFIRMED — from two independent sides Company release 2026-06-12: a six-year AI infrastructure compute collaboration with NVIDIA enabling 72MW of new datacenter capacity in Australia, deploying NVIDIA's DSX AI factory design with up to 40,000 Grace Blackwell GB300 GPUs, underpinned by a Master Cloud Services Agreement worth up to US$4.88 billion, structured as a revenue-sharing and credit-support model in which "Sharon AI will sell NVIDIA-powered cloud services, and NVIDIA will earn both standard product revenue and a share of the cloud revenue on the supported capacity." James Manning is quoted by name. The 10-Q corroborates the arrangement and its ~$4 billion scale and confirms use of proceeds. Use $4.88 billion for the MCSA ceiling and about $4 billion for the accounting figure — they are different measures, not a contradiction NVIDIA collaboration release
11 The GPU deployment target is more than 62,000 NVIDIA GPUs by mid-2027 CONFIRMED — with an as-of date attached 10-Q: "the Company had total AI Factory capacity of approximately 132MW, of which 116MW had been contracted to end customers, with deployment of more than 62,000 NVIDIA GPUs expected by mid-2027." Note the number has moved: the 2026-06-12 NVIDIA release said "more than 55,000 total NVIDIA GPUs deployed by mid-2027" against 132MW total with 102MW contracted. Both are correct as at their dates; contracted capacity rose from 102MW to 116MW and the GPU target rose with it. Quote 62,000 with the 2026-06-30 as-of date attached Form 10-Q
12 The GreenSquareDC relationship is real CONFIRMED — from both directions 10-Q: "At June 30, 2026, the Company has remaining contracted capacity of 132 MW, including a 15 MW commitment under a long-term data center services agreement entered into in March 2026 with GreenSquareDC Entity for a facility located in Australia. 13 The New Zealand capacity is real CONFIRMED — but it is contracted capacity, not operating capacity 10-Q: "The Company has also secured additional data center capacity in New Zealand for 14MW to support the deployment of cloud computing infrastructure under a long-term customer service agreement with commencement expected in Q1 2027. 14 The 2,048 NVIDIA B300 GPU deployment under the US$373 million agreement is hosted on New Zealand infrastructure (prior pass) CONTRADICTED The company's own 2026-08-04 release for that agreement places the initial 2,048 NVIDIA Blackwell Ultra B300 deployment in AUSTRALIA, across Sharon AI's AI infrastructure — not New Zealand. Two distinct agreements have been conflated: the US$1.32 billion five-year cloud computing service agreement announced 2026-07-16, which is the one associated with New Zealand capacity and an unnamed global AI lab; and the separate US$373 million five-year AI cloud service agreement announced 2026-08-04 with a global AI platform. The company's release for the second explicitly presents it as a new agreement separate from the first. Do not merge the two contract values or their locations US$373m release
15 The Delaware UCC search path is broken (HTTP 404), implying it might work on a retry CONTRADICTED — the 404 is a symptom, not the cause Re-running the portal will never succeed. Delaware operates NO free public online UCC debtor search. corp.delaware.gov/uccsearch/ states: "Effective December 1, 2001, all non 'Search to Reflect' UCC Searches will be performed by a Delaware Authorized Searcher" — a paid commercial intermediary from a published certified list. Five URL variants were probed this pass and all returned HTTP 404 because the endpoint does not exist for the public. The correct next action is to commission a Delaware Authorized Searcher against the five Delaware debtors (SharonAI Holdings Inc., SharonAI Inc., SharonAI Operations LLC, SharonAI Hosting LLC, SAI US No. 1 LLC), not to retry the website. The prior warning stands and should be repeated: zero UCC filings were read, and that must never be entered anywhere as "no liens found" Delaware UCC search notice

4. Leadership & ownership

Evidence grades used in this section: primary-record = SEC filing, internet-number registry, court docket, campaign-finance filing, or the company's own published page · corroborated = two or more independent sources agree · single-source = one source only, no corroboration · GAP = nothing found after a named search.

Three framing facts before the table. First, this company publishes an unusually complete named roster — the DEF 14A filed 2026-07-13 carries a full officer and director table with biographies, and sharonai.com/about/ carries a twenty-one-person management directory with individual bio pages. Names are not the problem here; reachability is. Exactly one individual email address is published for any Sharon AI employee — Ross.barrows@sharonai.com — and nineteen LinkedIn URLs are published by the company itself. Second, registry officers and network contacts are listed as rows in their own right and labelled as such — with the important negative finding that in this account the network registries yield no human beings at all: every ARIN and APNIC contact is a role account, and the PeeringDB poc_set is empty. That is a genuine finding, not a research failure. Third, a hard warning about a false contact route: every one of the nineteen management bio pages displays 123example@gmail.com and @123example. It is an unreplaced site-template placeholder, identical on all pages including the CEO's. It is not a contact route and must never be recorded as one.

Delaware therefore operates NO free public online UCC debtor search — it is a paid intermediary service. Five candidate URLs were probed; icis.corp.delaware.gov/Ecorp/UccSearch/UccSearchEntity.aspx and four variants all returned HTTP 404. The block is structural, not a broken link, and no amount of retrying the portal will fix it Attempted 2026-08-11 corp.delaware.gov/uccsearch

4.3 Buying committee

Unlike a founder-owned private host, this company has a full board, an audit and risk committee, a newly appointed Chief Legal Officer and a CFO transition in progress. There is a real procurement layer, and it is changing hands right now. Approach order matters more here than in any other account in this set — and the single most important change since the last pass is that there is now a named, US-scoped executive to call first.

Name Why they matter for a server purchase How to approach
Wolf (Wolfgang) Schubert — Country Manager – USA THE ANSWER TO "WHO SIGNS IN THE US" — and the single biggest change to this account since the last pass. He is the former CEO (Business Combination close to 2026-01-22), whose CEO employment agreement was with SharonAI Operations LLC, the Delaware subsidiary, and who now holds the explicitly US-scoped executive title. He retains 226,176 shares. His stated remit is to "support Sharon AI's US development and Asia-Pacific hyperscale, AI lab, and global enterprise sales opportunities". A former CEO who stayed on in a country role keeps internal standing that a normal country manager does not have The correct first call for a US-originated vendor conversation, and the only executive whose title makes US engagement his job. His background is Goldman Sachs / J.P. Morgan / Merrill Lynch / Silver Point / Strategic Value Partners (CRO) / Vista Equity / BlockFi — capital markets and risk, not engineering. Pitch landed cost per deployed GPU, delivery certainty against hard RFS dates, supply-base concentration risk and financing structure, then ask him to sponsor you into Mons. Two decades of HPC infrastructure across 10 countries, hands-on cluster administration, security and systems architecture; BSc Computer Science, University of Queensland. For a neocloud this is itself informative — the company communicates through SEC filings, Businesswire and trade press, not through operator communities, so there is no informal technical back-channel into it. · GAP — county property and assessor records. Not applicable in any useful sense: every operating facility is leased colocation in Australia or New Zealand, the Texas Odessa/Ector County build was sold on 2026-01-13, and the only US addresses are a Fifth Avenue suite and the unverified ARIN entry at 201 Clark Street, Sharon PA. The company owns no US real property that a US assessor would record.

Delaware Division of Corporations entity name search — unlike the prior pass, a valid __VIEWSTATE (596 bytes), __VIEWSTATEGENERATOR and __EVENTVALIDATION were extracted and a full authenticated-session POST for "SHARONAI" was submitted. Server returned HTTP 200 with "An error occurred while processing the request" and zero rows, because of the two CAPTCHA controls. CAPTCHAs are not solved. The block is now diagnosed rather than merely observed. · Delaware UCC — BLOCKED STRUCTURALLY. Five URL variants probed, all HTTP 404. Root cause: since 2001-12-01 all non-"Search to Reflect" UCC searches must be performed by a Delaware Authorized Searcher. There is no free public online UCC debtor search in Delaware; retrying the portal is futile. · Texas — sos.state.tx.us/ucc and direct.sos.state.tx.us are account-gated with a standing technical-difficulties notice; mycpa.cpa.state.tx.us/coa/ 302-redirects to a JavaScript-rendered page and POSTs returned the same 130,041-byte shell with no result rows. That pairing is the governance question worth planting with the Audit and Risk Management Committee; see §14.

6. Hardware fleet

Evidence grades used here: confirmed = first-hand named disclosure in an SEC filing or the company's own page, or multiple independent corroboration · circumstantial = behaviour or a panel listing points somewhere but no purchasing relationship is evidenced · inferred = derived only from a brand marker, socket or form factor · GAP = nothing found either way.

Vendor / category Evidence grade What the evidence actually says
World Wide Technology — WWT Australia Pty Ltd CONFIRMED — the incumbent integrator and the single largest hardware counterparty 10-Q Note 10: "prepayments primarily relate to deposits and milestone payments for high-performance computing equipment with WWT Australia Pty Ltd" — carrying $256,186 thousand ($256.2 MILLION) of equipment prepayments as of 2026-06-30. WWT is also named in the S-1 among the strategic partnerships forming the integrated AI ecosystem. This is the channel through which HPC equipment is actually being procured
Lenovo — Lenovo Group Limited; Lenovo Global Financial Services (Australia & New Zealand) Pty Limited CONFIRMED (commercial relationship) — five independent confirmations (1) 10-Q Note 10: "down payments under an Equipment-as-a-Service (EaaS)/Device-as-a-Service (DaaS) arrangement with Lenovo Global Financial Services (Australia & New Zealand) Pty Limited" — $33,094 thousand prepaid. (2) 10-Q commitments: "On December 12, 2025, the Company entered into a Statement of Work with Lenovo Global Financial Services (Australia & New Zealand) Pty Limited for managed infrastructure services… term of 60 months… upfront payment representing approximately 50% of the total contract value." (3) XBRL tag SHAZ:LenovoGlobalFinancialServicesLimitedMember appears in the 10-Q. (4) The S-1 names Lenovo Group Limited among strategic technology partnerships. (5) The company's own published node specs list "24 x 32GB TruDDR5 4800MHz RAM" (sharonai.com/ai-cloud/). Note the structure: hardware is consumed as a financed service, not bought outright — a significant competitive moat
Lenovo ThinkSystem SR675 V3 as the specific published node platform INFERRED — strong, but an inference, not a disclosure TruDDR5 is Lenovo's proprietary memory brand name, so its presence in the published spec points to Lenovo ThinkSystem hardware of the SR675 V3 class rather than white-box or any other OEM. The company never names a chassis or system model anywhere. Stated as an inference on purpose — the commercial Lenovo relationship is confirmed; this specific platform identification is not
Cisco Systems Inc. CONFIRMED — and a direct server competitor in this account Named in the S-1 among strategic partnerships: "Cisco provides the Company with AI-ready networking infrastructure, including access to Cisco Nexus HyperFabric AI and NVIDIA Spectrum-X-aligned" infrastructure. The company is "a strategic partner of Cisco" and "a member of the Cisco 360 Partner Program", which "authorizes us to build, sell, and manage integrated Cisco solutions." The B300 cluster at NEXTDC S3 is "built in partnership with Cisco and is expected to be Australia's first Cisco Secure AI Factory." The Digital Alpha press release specifies Cisco UCS servers, Cisco Nexus Hyperfabric switching, and the Cisco security and observability stack. Digital Alpha Advisors LLC — which holds an exclusive technology partnership with Cisco — invested up to US$200M and is a shareholder, which structurally locks Cisco in
NVIDIA Corporation CONFIRMED — and the party that effectively controls the OEM shortlist Six-year AI infrastructure compute collaboration entered 2026-06-12; ~$4 billion procurement commitment; up to 40,000 GB300 GPUs; NVIDIA DSX AI factory design; revenue-sharing and credit-support model. The company is a certified NVIDIA Cloud Partner (NCP) with visibility into NVIDIA's 12-to-18-month rolling supply chain forecast and preferential access to B200/B300 (10-Q; S-1)
AMD CONFIRMED — two distinct relationships CPU: published node specs use "2 x AMD EPYC 32C 3.25GHz CPUs" in both the H100 NVL and L40S nodes. GPU: AMD MI300X was offered in the public GPU catalogue at $2.90/GPU/hr (sharonai.com/ai-cloud/; Wayback 2024-12-08)
VAST Data Inc. (procured via Dicker Data Limited) CONFIRMED 10-Q Note 10: "prepayments to Dicker Data Limited for VAST Data software" — $13,368 thousand. 600PB VAST AI Operating System deployment announced 2026-06-16. Dicker Data is the Australian distributor of record
Megaport Limited CONFIRMED Named in the S-1 among the strategic partnerships forming the integrated AI ecosystem (connectivity / interconnect). Corroborated by PeeringDB: AS142588 peers at MegaIX Melbourne, 103.26.71.53, 10Gbps
NEXTDC Limited (ASX: NXT) CONFIRMED Primary, non-exclusive colocation provider. "we rely on NEXTDC to host substantially all of our GPU infrastructure" and "we have secured up to 87MW of capacity through NEXTDC." Not a server vendor, but the facility gatekeeper (10-Q)
Andrew Sjoquist Enterprises (ASE) CONFIRMED Managed service provider engaged under a noncancelable commitment of approximately AUD$400 thousand over five years, entered in connection with the termination of the Distributed Storage Solutions (DSS) data center services arrangement; supports the New Zealand deployment (10-Q)
Super Micro Computer, Inc. CIRCUMSTANTIAL — DO NOT OVERSTATE THIS. Never state it as a purchasing relationship. Supermicro appears exactly once across the entire 10-Q, DEF 14A, three 8-Ks and the 4.6 MB S-1, and it is not as a purchasing relationship. The single reference sits in a list of benefits derived from NVIDIA Cloud Partner status: "OEM Relationships: Facilitated engagement with OEMs such as Cisco, Lenovo, Dell, and Super Micro to support server procurement and integration" (S-1). That is NVIDIA facilitating access to a panel of OEMs. It establishes that Supermicro is inside the approved and known OEM set, and nothing more. There is no purchase order, no prepayment, no commitment, no named agreement, and no Supermicro hardware in any published node spec. All published node specs point to Lenovo. Grade is circumstantial, not confirmed
Dell Technologies CIRCUMSTANTIAL The same single S-1 sentence as Supermicro: "OEM Relationships: Facilitated engagement with OEMs such as Cisco, Lenovo, Dell, and Super Micro". No purchase evidence anywhere. Listed as a known OEM channel via NVIDIA NCP status only (S-1)
Hewlett Packard Enterprise (HPE), Gigabyte, ASUS, Penguin Computing, Pure Storage, WEKA, Arista GAP Explicitly searched for across the 10-Q, DEF 14A, S-1, careers page and product pages. Zero occurrences of any of these vendors. No evidence either way — recorded as a genuine absence rather than a negative finding

7.3 AI position

The AI position is the whole company — this is not a general-purpose host with a GPU line attached. The S-1 describes providing "enterprise, government and research organizations with sovereign, low-latency access to advanced accelerated computing hardware", targeting "AI startups, enterprises, and university researchers" and "research, government, and enterprise customers requiring GPU resources for LLM training, fine-tuning, and real-time inference." NEXTDC's Australian Federal Government Digital Transformation Agency (DTA) "Certified Strategic" status is cited as enabling sovereign government workloads, and NVIDIA's "consumption desk" refers qualified customers to the company.

The generation ladder, read off the evidence: A40 / L40 / L40S / RTX3090 (legacy) → H100 NVL (~Sep 2024) → H200 (~Dec 2024, now the installed 1,016-GPU Supercluster) → B200 (balance of Supercluster, 1H 2026) → B300 (1,024 units at NEXTDC S3, 1H 2026; a further 2,048 from Q1 2027) → GB300, up to 40,000 units, April–August 2027. There is no generation hole here — unlike a legacy host, this operator tracks NVIDIA's cadence almost immediately, which is consistent with NCP status and the stated visibility into NVIDIA's 12-to-18-month rolling supply forecast. The opportunity is not a refresh gap. It is the sheer size of the forward build.

8. Purchase clock

How often this company actually commits to new silicon, reconstructed from three independent series, none of which relies on a vendor claim.

8.2 Cadence

Two overlapping clocks, and the second has taken over.

The CATALOGUE clock ran at roughly one new NVIDIA generation every 3–6 months through 2024 — L40S/A40 → H100 NVL (Sep 24) → H200 (Dec 24) — tracking NVIDIA's release cadence almost immediately. That is consistent with NCP status and the stated visibility into NVIDIA's 12-to-18-month rolling supply forecast.

The CAPITAL clock is now dominant and faster: four financing events in seven months — December 2025, January 2026, April/May 2026, June 2026 — each immediately earmarked for GPU and infrastructure procurement. Since going public the company has moved from roughly $100k-scale catalogue refreshes to ~$765.1M + ~$217.2M committed and ~$4bn contracted. Effective procurement cadence is now roughly quarterly and event-driven by capital raises rather than by product cycle.

8.3 Last event and next window

Last event: 2026-06-12 — the NVIDIA six-year collaboration (~$4bn commitment, up to 40,000 GB300), funded by the $900M equity plus convertible note raise that closed 2026-06-22/23. Most recent incremental commitment: ~$217.2 million for 2,048 NVIDIA B300 units, disclosed as a subsequent event in the 10-Q filed 2026-08-06.

Next window — two, and they are different in kind.

  • NEAR: approximately August – November 2026. This covers the ~29.6MW of third-party capacity contracted in May 2026 that commences "late 2026", the GreenSquareDC 15MW with a Ready-for-Service target of 2026-09-26, and the New Zealand 14MW with Q1 2027 commencement.
  • PRIMARY: approximately Q4 2026 through Q2 2027, for the B300 and GB300 tranches. This is estimated by working back from the disclosed April–August 2027 five-phase NEXTDC RFS dates and the Q1 2027 revenue start, minus a typical two-to-three-quarter procurement and integration lead time for rack-scale Blackwell. Labelled an estimate on purpose.
  • A secondary soft window opens around October – November 2026, when incoming CFO Anuj Goel (starting 2026-08-24) completes his first vendor and commitment review. This is the one to work.

8.4 Method

Triangulation of three independent series, none of which relies on a vendor claim.

(a) Wayback Machine CDX enumeration of sharonai.com/cloud-pricing/ — 14 distinct captures, 2024-09-19 to 2026-03-28 — fetching raw id_ snapshots and extracting the SKU / price / availability table. The "Reserve Now (Sep 24)" and "Reserve Now (Dec 24)" labels date the purchase commitments directly, and the transition of a SKU from "Reserve Now" to "Available Now" dates the installation. (b) SEC-disclosed deployment milestones and Ready-for-Service dates from the 10-Q commitments note and the S-1 business section. (c) SEC-disclosed financing closings from the 8-K series, on the premise — stated explicitly in the 8-K use-of-proceeds language — that each raise is earmarked for specific hardware.

Purchase windows are then estimated as disclosed RFS date minus two to three quarters.

CAVEAT, stated plainly: the (a) series terminates in mid-2025 because the company withdrew public pricing, so the post-2025 clock rests on (b) and (c) only and is correspondingly less granular. There is no catalogue-level visibility into 2026 purchasing at all; everything after mid-2025 is inferred from disclosed commitments and capital events.

9. UCC financing record

Scope of this track: the four Delaware entities — SharonAI Holdings Inc. (parent), SharonAI Inc., SharonAI Operations LLC, SharonAI Hosting LLC, SAI US No. 1 LLC — plus, by reference, the Australian entities SharonAI Pty Ltd and Distributed Storage Solutions Pty Ltd.

9.1 Verdict

UNVERIFIED — portal blocked

Read that exactly as written. Zero UCC-1 financing statements were retrieved, transcribed, or ruled out. Zero filings were read. No filing number, filing date, lapse or continuation status, secured party, debtor, collateral description, or amendment / assignment / termination data can be reported, because no search was successfully executed against any UCC index. The empty filing set in §9.3 means nothing was seen. It does not mean this company has no secured debt. It must never be reported to a customer, quoted to credit, or entered into CRM as "no liens found".

One jurisdictional point that matters more than the block itself: the state of organization is DELAWARE for the parent and for all four US subsidiaries, so Delaware is the correct filing office under UCC 9-301 / 9-307. Texas is NOT the state of organization, and a Texas UCC search would not be dispositive even if it had succeeded. Anyone re-running this must go to Delaware, not Texas.

9.4 What the record means

Inference, not sighted evidence. Read this section as "not looked at", not as "no liens".

That said, the SEC filings independently reveal a great deal about how this company funds hardware, and the picture that emerges makes traditional UCC-1 equipment liens less central than they would be for a typical self-funded GPU operator. Four funding mechanisms are documented, and none of them is a classic secured equipment loan.

Observation What it implies Confidence
(1) EQUITY AND UNSECURED CONVERTIBLES. A $900M equity raise (June 2026), $350M of 6.00% convertible senior notes due 2031 (May 2026), and a 4.75% convertible note issue (June 2026). The June 2026 notes are expressly "fully and unconditionally guaranteed on a SENIOR UNSECURED basis by the Subsidiary Guarantors" Unsecured means no UCC-1 attaches. The constraint is the indenture's negative covenants, not a filed security interest. This is the single biggest reason a UCC search here would likely return less than one expects high — directly quoted from the 8-K
(2) VENDOR-FINANCED CONSUMPTION. The Lenovo Global Financial Services EaaS/DaaS arrangement plus a 60-month managed-services Statement of Work with ~50% paid upfront Under an equipment-as-a-service structure the financier typically retains title, so the exposure appears as a service commitment rather than as a debtor-side security interest. Where Lenovo GFS does perfect, expect precautionary filings naming a Lenovo entity as secured party medium — the arrangement is directly disclosed; the title/perfection reading is commercial inference
(3) PREPAYMENT. $256.2M prepaid to WWT Australia and $33.1M to Lenovo GFS Paying cash in advance is the opposite of borrowing against the asset and generates no lien at all. It also concentrates counterparty risk in a way a new CFO will notice high — the prepayment balances are directly disclosed in 10-Q Note 10
(4) NVIDIA REVENUE-SHARE AND CREDIT SUPPORT. The six-year collaboration aligns economics through "a revenue-sharing and credit-support model", described by the company as a capital-efficient path to scale This displaces conventional debt financing for the largest single tranche of hardware, and gives NVIDIA structural influence over the OEM shortlist medium-high — the structure is disclosed; the influence conclusion is inference
JURISDICTION. The counterparties are overwhelmingly Australian — WWT Australia Pty Ltd, Lenovo GFS Australia & New Zealand Pty Limited, Dicker Data Limited, SharonAI Pty Ltd The meaningful security interests would be registered on the Australian PPSR, not in any US UCC index. A US search, even if it had succeeded, would likely have found comparatively little medium-high — the counterparty entities are named in the filings; the PPSR conclusion follows from where they are incorporated
COMMERCIAL CONSEQUENCE. Because the hardware is substantially consumed as a financed service rather than purchased outright A capex proposal is competing against an opex structure. That is a materially harder commercial comparison than a like-for-like box quote, and it should be understood before any pricing work is done medium — follows from the disclosed EaaS/DaaS structure

RECOMMENDED FOLLOW-UP, in priority order: 1. Run the Australian PPSR against SharonAI Pty Ltd and Distributed Storage Solutions Pty Ltd. This is the highest-yield missing search in the entire dossier. 2. Run a paid Delaware UCC search against all four Delaware entities. 3. Read the June 2026 Indenture (Exhibit 4.2) for the lien and permitted-indebtedness covenants, which will define how much secured equipment financing they are contractually allowed to add — and therefore whether a structured, capex-style proposal is even available to them.

10. Cost ceiling

What a node can be worth to this operator, and what the same box looks like rebuilt. Every figure in this section is a modelled estimate built on assumptions I chose. None of it came from Sharon AI, and none of it should be presented to the customer as their economics.

10.1 Assumptions — read these first; they are assumptions, not findings

Stated explicitly so they can be challenged:

  1. Realised rate $1.50–$2.50/GPU/hr, against a $2.50 list anchor for H100 NVL from the 2024-12-08 rate card. The band assumes 0–40% off list, since the site says "Contact us for volume and term discount" and the company now sells via take-or-pay contracts.
  2. Paid utilisation 60–80%.
  3. Operating-cost share 35–45% of revenue, covering colocation, power, cooling, network and support but NOT hardware. These are the exact cost categories the 10-Q itself names: "data center costs — comprising colocation facility fees, internet connectivity, and power consumption."
  4. 8 GPUs per node, per the company's own published spec.
  5. 8,760 hours per year.
  6. Payback measured on hardware acquisition cost only, excluding integration, spares and freight.
  7. No residual value credited.
  8. No financing cost — which is conservative in the wrong direction, given the company actually consumes hardware via a Lenovo EaaS/DaaS structure with an embedded finance charge.

TWO HEALTH WARNINGS. First, the price anchor is roughly 20 months stale — public pricing was withdrawn during 2025 and the current commercial model is bespoke mega-contracts ($373M, $950M, $1.32bn), so the true realised rate is unknown. Second, the 10-Q explicitly flags that "rising operational costs, particularly for power, colocation services, and network infrastructure, are increasing the cost base for GPU cloud providers", which pushes the operating-cost share toward the upper end of the assumed band and the ceiling toward the lower end.

10.2 Rent-derived ceiling — 8 × H100 NVL node

Line Low case High case
List gross at 100% utilisation (8 × $2.50 × 8,760) $175,200 / node / year — nobody realises this —
Annual gross revenue per node (8 × rate × 8,760 × utilisation) $63,072 (8 × $1.50 × 8,760 × 0.60) $140,160 (8 × $2.50 × 8,760 × 0.80)
Less assumed operating-cost share (45% low case / 35% high case) −$28,382 −$49,056
Available to service hardware cost, per node per year $34,690 $91,104
Indicative hardware cost ceiling @ 12-month payback $34,690 $91,104
Indicative hardware cost ceiling @ 18-month payback $52,035 $136,656
Indicative hardware cost ceiling @ 24-month payback $69,380 $182,208

Read the 24-month figure as the realistic commercial ceiling, since neocloud contracts here run five years and the NEXTDC lease term is 120 months. A 24-month payback is a hurdle rate, not an asset-life constraint.

10.3 BOM rebuild — structure verified, street pricing NOT sourced

The flagship node is published verbatim by the company (sharonai.com/ai-cloud/):

Component Published specification Note
GPU 8 × H100 NVL (94GB) with NVLink Bridge (188GB) Will dominate at roughly 80–85% of node cost
CPU 2 × AMD EPYC 32C 3.25GHz The page renders as "3954" — almost certainly EPYC 9354. Inferred, not disclosed
Memory 24 × 32GB TruDDR5 4800MHz (768GB total) TruDDR5 is Lenovo's proprietary memory brand — the platform tell
Storage (data) 6 × 7.68TB U.2 NVMe (~46TB usable)
Storage (OS) 2 × 1.92TB M.2 NVMe (3.84TB)
Second published node 8 × L40S (48GB) with an otherwise identical CPU, memory and storage configuration

PLATFORM IDENTIFICATION: "TruDDR5" indicates a Lenovo ThinkSystem PCIe GPU node, SR675 V3 class. Inferred from a brand marker, not disclosed.

SUPERMICRO CROSSWALK: the direct equivalent is the AS-4125GS-TNRT (4U, 8 × PCIe double-width GPU, dual AMD EPYC 9004/9005, 24 DIMM slots, NVMe front bays) — a genuine like-for-like on GPU count, CPU socket type, DIMM count and drive topology, which makes it a clean side-by-side. For the L40S node the same chassis or the SYS-421GE-TNRT applies.

HONEST GAP: street pricing was NOT sourced in this pass for the H100 NVL cards, the EPYC 9354 CPUs, the DDR5 RDIMMs, the U.2/M.2 NVMe, or the Supermicro chassis — and none were invented. The BOM comparison is therefore structurally complete and financially incomplete.

To close it: price the eight H100 NVL cards first — they will dominate at roughly 80–85% of node cost and therefore decide the comparison almost on their own — then the chassis-plus-CPU-plus-memory-plus-storage remainder, and compare against a configured AS-4125GS-TNRT quote.

And note the structural constraint this reveals: the GPU cards are largely vendor-neutral in cost, so the winnable delta is confined to the ~15–20% of node value represented by the chassis, board, power, cooling and integration. That is precisely the argument that has to be made against an incumbent who is already financing the whole stack as a service — which is a much harder argument than a box-price comparison, and it should be understood before anyone builds a quote.

14. Supermicro sales angle

Classification: mis-territoried account → escalate to APAC/Australia coverage. If worked at all, it is a hard displacement, not greenfield.

HONEST VERDICT FIRST. This is not a viable Team 1 USA opportunity, and the CRM-clean, registrable status is misleading rather than encouraging. The registrable US entity is a Delaware holding shell with a New York registered office; the buying entity is SharonAI Pty Ltd in Australia; the entire fleet sits in NEXTDC Melbourne and Sydney plus GreenSquareDC and New Zealand; every IXP and facility in PeeringDB is Australian; and the Texas datacenter JV that presumably justified the T1 designation was SOLD on 2026-01-13. Registering this as a T1 lead on the strength of a Houston address that does not appear in any source would be mis-territoried, and the $100M threshold would be met by an opportunity that a US team cannot service or close. Do not burn one of the five CRM slots on it as a T1 lead.

The wedge, if the account is worked through the correct geography

DISPLACEMENT, and a hard one. This is not greenfield.

  • Lenovo is the confirmed incumbent commercial relationship on the compute platform — the published node spec carries "TruDDR5", Lenovo's proprietary memory brand, and there is a 60-month managed-services SOW with ~50% paid upfront plus $33.1M of EaaS/DaaS down payments (10-Q; sharonai.com/ai-cloud/).
  • WWT Australia holds $256.2M of equipment prepayments as integrator of record.
  • Cisco is locked in structurally, not just commercially — Digital Alpha, which holds an exclusive technology partnership with Cisco, invested up to $200M and is a shareholder, and the flagship B300 cluster is branded "Australia's first Cisco Secure AI Factory".
  • NVIDIA controls the OEM shortlist through NCP status, DSX reference designs, and a revenue-share plus credit-support structure.

Four separate parties therefore have a commercial interest in keeping the current OEM mix intact. That is the real obstacle — not price, and not product.

The one genuine opening

The company is consuming hardware as a financed service (EaaS/DaaS) while carrying $256.2M prepaid to a single integrator, against ~$4bn of committed procurement and a 70-person team. That is concentrated supply-chain and prepayment risk sitting on a balance sheet that just raised $900M. A new CFO, Anuj Goel, starts 2026-08-24 and will own that risk.

The credible pitch is supply-base diversification and delivery certainty for the April–August 2027 five-phase NEXTDC ramp — not price, and not features. The realistic claim is on the ~15–20% of node value that is not the GPU cards, plus lead-time and rack-integration capacity for GB300 NVL72 at a scale where Lenovo and Cisco allocation may be constrained.

Never state that Supermicro is an incumbent here. The grade is circumstantial — one sentence in the S-1, listing an OEM panel made available through NVIDIA NCP status. Anyone who opens with "we know you're running Supermicro" will be corrected within one sentence.

Qualifying questions for first contact

To Daniel Mons (CTO), or via Peter Woodward:

"For the April-to-August 2027 NEXTDC phases, is your GB300 rack-scale allocation single-sourced through WWT and Lenovo, or are you holding a second OEM lane open for schedule protection?"

This is diagnostic rather than pitchy — it surfaces whether a second source is even contractually permitted under the NVIDIA revenue-share and credit-support arrangement, which is the real gating question.

A useful second probe:

"Does the Lenovo EaaS structure let you take title on any tranche, or is all capacity consumption-based?"

If the answer is that everything is service-consumed, a capex proposal cannot compete and the account should be deprioritised. That is a legitimate outcome and should be recorded as one.

Rule 8 — distributor caution (read before dialling)

Do not register Sharon AI as an end-user deal. The transacting party will not be Sharon AI directly — hardware flows through WWT Australia Pty Ltd (integrator, $256.2M prepaid), Lenovo Global Financial Services Australia & New Zealand Pty Limited (financier), and Dicker Data Limited (distributor, VAST software). Registering an end-user that a distributor or integrator already controls creates channel conflict and risks colliding with an existing partner-side registration.

Confirm the Australian distribution path and check for an existing APAC partner claim BEFORE any registration attempt.

Cross-team co-op applies here: per standing practice, do not kill this on HQ location alone. Hand it to the team that owns the geography where the buying actually happens, and keep it revivable rather than closing it out.

Sequence — do not reorder: ① correct the territory record (the Houston address is unsupported; TCDC was sold 2026-01-13) → ② hand off to APAC/Australia coverage and check for an existing partner-side claim → ③ clear the channel position under Rule 8 → ④ only then, if the geography team wants support, supply the two qualifying questions above.

15.3 Open gaps

  1. UCC-1 financing statements — ZERO filings retrieved. The Delaware UCC search path returned HTTP 404; the Delaware entity name search is CAPTCHA-gated; Texas SOSDirect and the SOS UCC Portal require paid accounts. Verdict recorded as UNVERIFIED — portal blocked, NOT as "no financing". Note also that Delaware, not Texas, is the correct filing office for all five US entities.
  2. Australian PPSR not searched — this is the HIGHEST-VALUE MISSING SOURCE. The real security interests almost certainly sit here, against SharonAI Pty Ltd and Distributed Storage Solutions Pty Ltd, because the counterparties (WWT Australia Pty Ltd, Lenovo GFS Australia & New Zealand Pty Limited, Dicker Data Limited) are all Australian. A US UCC search would not find them even if it had succeeded.
  3. FEC individual-contribution records — NOT verified for any principal. OpenFEC returned OVER_RATE_LIMIT on DEMO_KEY across all attempts; fec.gov's UI is JS-rendered. Explicitly NOT recorded as "no record found". Peter Woodward and Benjamin Adams — the two US-based directors — are the priority re-runs and need a registered API key.
  4. USPTO trademark data — nothing obtained. No registration, serial number, declaration signatory or correspondent. tmsearch.uspto.gov returned S3 NoSuchKey / MethodNotAllowed; developer.uspto.gov redirected; Justia returned no parseable rows. Whether "SHARON AI" is even registered as a US mark is unknown.
  5. Delaware registered agent, incorporators and annual-report signatories — not obtained (CAPTCHA-gated portal).
  6. Historical WHOIS — no pre-privacy registrant recovered. Current WHOIS is privacy-shielded ("Domain Privacy", PO Box 119, Beaconsfield VIC 3807). whoisrequest.com/history, whoxy.com and securitytrails were not successfully queried; these are largely paid services. No founder-era registrant name.
  7. LinkedIn profiles — not resolved for ANY named individual. Thirteen principals, zero profile URLs, no headcount-by-function, and no identification of US-based staff beyond the single Austin TX Technical Accountant posting.
  8. Street pricing for the BOM rebuild — NOT sourced. Node composition is verified verbatim and the Supermicro crosswalk (AS-4125GS-TNRT) is identified, but no component prices were researched and none were invented. The BOM comparison is structurally complete and financially incomplete.
  9. Current GPU rate card — DOES NOT EXIST PUBLICLY. /cloud-pricing/ returns zero bytes and /pricing-calculator/ 301-redirects to the homepage as of 2026-08-11. All captured prices are from October and December 2024 and are ~20 months stale. Current realised pricing under the bespoke mega-contracts is unknown, which materially weakens the rent-derived cost ceiling.
  10. Named end customers — NONE disclosed. Both mega-contracts ($1.32bn AI lab, $950M technology company) are described only by category. No customer-concentration table exists in the 10-Q.
  11. County property and assessor records — not searched. Now largely moot for Ector County (TCDC divested 2026-01-13) and inapplicable to the Australian and New Zealand colocation footprint, which is leased from NEXTDC and GreenSquareDC rather than owned.
  12. Michael Schubert's given name — not verified verbatim.
  13. Exact Austin, TX address and headcount — not obtained.
  14. Indenture covenants (Exhibit 4.2, June 2026) — NOT READ. These define permitted liens and permitted indebtedness and would determine how much secured equipment financing the company can legally add — which directly affects whether a capex-based proposal is even structurally available to them. This is the second-highest-value missing document after the PPSR.
  15. AS142588 IP prefixes — CIDR blocks not enumerated. PeeringDB reports 6 IPv4 and 1 IPv6 prefix but the specific blocks were not pulled from APNIC.
  16. No named individual NOC or network staff exist in any public registry. PeeringDB poc_set is empty and all APNIC contacts (SS4419-AP, SNA102-AP, IRT-DSSL-AU) are role accounts, not people. This is a genuine negative finding rather than a research failure — but it means the named staff that network sources were expected to yield do not exist here.
  17. Fetch and tooling notes for a re-run — WebFetch against sec.gov returns HTTP 403; use curl with a compliant SEC User-Agent. The SEC EDGAR archive directory listing for the S-1 accession returned navigation chrome rather than a file index, so Exhibit 21 was not retrieved. The Texas Comptroller search and the fec.gov contributions search are both JavaScript applications and cannot be queried programmatically. The ZoomInfo MCP connector (and carta, figma, atlassian, spglobal, adobe connectors) require OAuth authorisation and could not be used in this non-interactive session; authorising ZoomInfo via claude.ai connector settings would likely close the LinkedIn and named-staff gaps on a re-run.

對表頭與原始工作簡報的四項更正 — 請先讀完這一段

上方表頭係按原文照錄。其中四項前提與一手證據相牴觸。 這裡選擇明列更正而非默默改寫，以便回頭修正原始名單的源頭。

  1. 並非私有持股。 本公司已於 2025-12-19 透過與 Roth CH Acquisition Co. 的 SPAC 合併上市，具 SEC 申報義務：CIK 0002068385、Commission File 001-43129、Nasdaq: SHAZ（A 類普通股）與 SHAZW（認股權證）。Form 10-Q、4.6 MB 的 Form S-1、DEF 14A 與多份 8-K 均存在且已閱讀。「預期沒有 SEC 申報」這項前提為誤，且這些申報文件是本檔中證據力最強的來源（EDGAR submissions API）。
  2. 並非休士頓。 登記在案的主要營業處所為 745 Fifth Avenue, Suite 500, New York, NY 10151。任何 SEC 申報、sharonai.com 或徵才頁上均未出現任何休士頓地址。全網唯一找到的美國實體據點，是規模不大的 德州 Austin 財會據點（EDGAR submissions API；Form 8-K 2026-07-24）。
  3. 德州資料中心已經沒了。 Texas Critical Data Centers LLC（TCDC）——原規劃位於 Ector County／Odessa、250 MW 的表後（behind-the-meter）案場——已於 2026-01-13 出售予合資夥伴 New Era Energy & Digital（NUAI）。10-Q 明載本公司「ceased to have an ownership interest in TCDC or participate in the joint venture」。德州資料中心題材已完全不存在。 這是本次最重要的一項更正（Form 10-Q，截至 2026-06-30 之季度）。
  4. 這實質上不是一組（美國）的硬體機會。 10-Q 自述本公司為「an Australian neocloud operator」。幾乎全部 GPU 基礎設施都放在 NEXTDC 的墨爾本與雪梨機房，另加 GreenSquareDC（澳洲）與紐西蘭。簽署 Lenovo Statement of Work 與高階主管聘僱契約的採購主體是澳洲公司 SharonAI Pty Ltd。PeeringDB 紀錄中的每一個 IXP 與機房據點都在澳洲，而且完全不存在 ARIN 組織紀錄。T1｜T3 這個轄區標記，掛在一個設有紐約登記辦公室的德拉瓦州控股殼公司上，而不是掛在一支會買硬體的機隊上。

以下內容不依賴任何已被更正的前提。凡簡報與證據不一致之處，均陳述證據並將簡報版本標記為「已被反證」，不逕行刪除。

1. 結論摘要

Sharon AI 是一家剛上市、資金極為充裕、實際營運在澳洲的 neocloud 業者——SharonAI Holdings Inc.（Nasdaq: SHAZ），CIK 0002068385，德拉瓦州設立、紐約登記辦公室，上半年實際營收 $2,225,396，對照的是約 22.7 億美元的已宣布合約金額與對 NVIDIA 約 40 億美元的採購承諾，而執行團隊只有 70 位員工、董事、顧問與約聘人員（Form 10-Q；Form S-1，2026-07-31）。先講對本組最關鍵、也最會改變後續動作的判斷：這不是一組（美國）可行的機會，而 CRM 乾淨這件事在此案中是誤導，而不是好消息。 可註冊的美國主體是一家控股殼公司；採購主體是澳洲的 SharonAI Pty Ltd；機隊在 NEXTDC M3（墨爾本） 與 NEXTDC S3（雪梨），加上 GreenSquareDC 與紐西蘭；而唯一的德州資產——當初很可能就是它造成 T1 標記的 250 MW Ector County 合資案——已於 2026-01-13 出售。

硬體面同樣清楚，也同樣不利。Lenovo 是運算平台上已確認的既有商業關係——2025-12-12 簽署、為期 60 個月、預付約 50% 合約金額的託管基礎設施 Statement of Work，$33,094 千美元的 Equipment-as-a-Service／Device-as-a-Service 頭期款，10-Q 中出現 Lenovo Global Financial Services 的 XBRL member 標籤，以及公司自家公布的節點規格中列有「24 x 32GB TruDDR5 4800MHz RAM」，而 TruDDR5 是 Lenovo 專有的記憶體品牌名稱（10-Q；sharonai.com/ai-cloud/）。World Wide Technology（WWT Australia Pty Ltd）是登記在案的系統整合商，截至 2026-06-30 帳上有 $256,186 千美元——2.562 億美元——的設備預付款。Cisco 是結構性鎖定，不只是商業合作：與 Cisco 具獨家技術合作關係的 Digital Alpha Advisors 投資最高 US$200M 並成為股東，而旗艦 B300 叢集被冠名為「Australia's first Cisco Secure AI Factory」（S-1；Digital Alpha 新聞稿）。

Supermicro 在整份證據集中只出現過一次——10-Q、DEF 14A、三份 8-K 與 4.6 MB 的 S-1——而且不是以採購關係出現。那唯一一次出現在 NVIDIA Cloud Partner 身分所帶來的利益清單裡：「OEM Relationships: Facilitated engagement with OEMs such as Cisco, Lenovo, Dell, and Super Micro to support server procurement and integration」（S-1）。這只能證明 Supermicro 屬於已認可的 OEM 名單，除此之外什麼都不能證明：沒有採購單、沒有預付款、沒有承諾、沒有具名合約，任何公開節點規格中也沒有 Supermicro 硬體。等級：情況證據（circumstantial）。對內不得聲稱 Supermicro 是本案既有供應商。

真正的切入點只有一個，而且是時機型而非產品型。本公司在以融資服務方式消費硬體的同時，還有 2.562 億美元預付給單一整合商，對照 約 7.651 億美元的近期採購承諾、另外 約 2.172 億美元的 2,048 顆 NVIDIA B300，以及與 NVIDIA 簽下的 約 40 億美元——而執行團隊只有 70 人。全新財務長 Anuj Goel 於 2026-08-24 到職，也就是本次研究日之後兩週，而這份集中度風險將由他承接。若真要經營此案，時點應落在 2026 年 10–11 月，透過正確的區域團隊，訴求 2027 年 4 至 8 月 NEXTDC 五階段上線的供應來源分散與交期確定性——不是價格，也不是規格。應轉交 APAC／澳洲團隊；不要把五個 CRM 名額之一花在 T1 註冊上。

3. 原始名單更正表

僅列與 Sharon AI 有關者。判定尺度：confirmed（已證實）＝第一手具名揭露或多方獨立佐證 · partly-confirmed（部分證實）＝核心屬實但框架需要修正 · contradicted（已被反證）＝證據與原始名單、或與本檔前一版相牴觸 · unverified（未經查證）＝無法由公開來源確立，不得對客戶陳述。

# 原始名單說法 判定 證據與正確說法 來源
1 集團母公司是澳洲公司，美國主體是 Sharon AI, Inc. CONTRADICTED（已被反證） 方向完全相反。集團母公司是 SharonAI Holdings Inc.，一家德拉瓦州公司 — CIK 0002068385、Commission File 001-43129、EIN 41-2349750、SIC 7374，主要營業處所 745 Fifth Avenue Suite 500, New York NY 10151，Nasdaq 代號 SHAZ，認股權證 SHAZW 於 OTC 交易。曾用名為「Roth CH Holdings, Inc.」與「SharonAI Holdings, Inc.」。10-Q 明載：「SharonAI Inc. (SA Inc.) is a digital infrastructure provider, incorporated in the state of Delaware in the United States of America on February 15, 2024」——因此簡報所稱的「美國主體」本身就是德拉瓦州公司，而且是全資子公司，不是母公司。SharonAI Pty Ltd 與 Distributed Storage Solutions Pty Ltd 是營運子公司兼子公司保證人。不存在澳洲母公司，也沒有 ASX 上市 — ASX 第二上市曾公開設為目標且公開延後，「Sharon AI Ltd」並不存在為一家上市實體 EDGAR submissions
2 Sharon AI 沒有 ARIN 組織紀錄，在美國完全沒有網路存在（本檔前一版自行陳述的反面發現） CONTRADICTED — 前一版最大的一項事實錯誤 ARIN 組織 SOL-111，名稱「SharonAi」確實存在，登記日 2024-06-27。其持有 AS401247（ASN 名稱 SHARON-AI，2024-07-12 登記）與 IPv6 區塊 2607:C5C0::/32（NET6-2607-C5C0-1，名稱 SHARON-AI，Direct Allocation，2024-07-17 登記）。這不是名稱巧合：兩個 ARIN POC — SNT23-ARIN 與 SAT42-ARIN — 的 companyName 皆為「SharonAI PTY LTD」，地址 303/44 Miller Street, North Sydney NSW 2060，與 APNIC 註冊人 ORG-SA242-AP 逐字元完全相同。前一版之所以漏掉，是因為以 Sharon* 查詢後把結果讀成不相關；相符的組織拼法是一個字的「SharonAi」，就在那批結果裡。該說法較窄的一半仍成立：沒有任何路由被通告。 RIPEstat 顯示 AS401247 未通告任何前綴，2607:C5C0::/32 的 announced=null，321 個 IPv6 RIS peer 中 0 個看得到。正確說法：Sharon AI 在 ARIN 持有休眠、未通告的美國號碼資源 ARIN org SOL-111
3 前任執行長是「Michael Schubert」（前一版，且已註明名字未經查證，僅呈現為「Mr. Schubert」） CONTRADICTED（已被反證） 他的姓名是 WOLFGANG SCHUBERT，慣稱 Wolf Schubert，而且人並未離開。DEF 14A 兩處逐字具名：受益所有權表（「Wolfgang Schubert 226,176」）與薪酬彙總表（「Wolfgang Schubert / Chief Executive Officer / 2024 salary 129,133 / 2025 salary 276,818」）。他現任 Country Manager – USA，於 sharonai.com 有專屬頁面與公開 LinkedIn。這件事的意義是商業的、不只是文書的：前一版把他註記為「僅供歷史參考 — 不得作為決策者接觸」的這個人，實際上是公司位階最高的美國本地主管，也是美方發起供應商接觸時最自然的第一通電話 sharonai.com/management/wolf-schubert · DEF 14A
4 每一位具名 principal 的 LinkedIn 都是未解出的 GAP — 十三位具名 principals、零個 LinkedIn URL（前一版） CONTRADICTED（已被反證） 公司自己在其管理層頁面上公布了十九個 LinkedIn URL，而且每一個都由 team-card 標記與其本人綁定，不是從網址代稱推測：mrjamesmanning、andrewjohnleece、nicholas-hughes-jones、dan-mons-1061b79、wolf-schubert-a9b6a5229、t-broadfoot、tim-flahvin-651b972、rossbarrows、lintonburling、kieranhabojan、kathleen-manning-6a97421b3、melissa-anastasiou-3918b514、andrew-penn-48707219、peter-woodward-6728493、alastair-cairns、drew-kelton-9364863b、benjamin-adams-7a438511、michael-e-mueller、lucb1。查證方法：十一個高階團隊 URL 在每一頁的「Other Team Members」輪播中重複出現，且每個 URL 在 DOM 中都緊接在該人姓名與職稱之後；五個董事 URL 只出現在該董事自己頁面的 hero 區塊。歸屬屬 primary-record，不是猜測 sharonai.com/about
5 因 OpenFEC API 流量受限，無法取得任何 principal 的 FEC 紀錄（前一版所述阻擋原因） CONTRADICTED — 阻擋屬實但可繞過，且現已解除 DEMO_KEY 在一小時內四次嘗試皆維持 OVER_RATE_LIMIT。可行路徑是 FEC 自家公開資料頁 fec.gov 內嵌的 api.data.gov key — 對每一位訪客都會送出，且可回傳完整 Schedule A 結果。隨後共完成約 四十筆查詢。結果：三位 principal 或其鄰接人士有真實且相符的聯邦紀錄（Alastair Cairns、Benjamin Adams，以及 19.9% 股東的 Nicholas Gross Whitaker），兩位為未經確認的同名相符（Carl Shulman、Henry Orren），二十位為已查證的空值。前一版當時的謹慎是正確的；但「未取得任何 FEC 紀錄」這個結論現已被取代 — 見第 12 節 FEC Schedule A — CAIRNS, ALASTAIR
6 James Manning 為持股逾 10% 之股東 CONTRADICTED（已被反證） 就股權而言並非如此。 他本人於 2026-03-30 申報的 Schedule 13D 載明，截至 2026-03-13 持有 1,442,696 股＝A 類股 8.95%。DEF 14A 受益所有權表則為 1,562,984 股 A 類＝4.57%。超過 10% 的是投票權控制 — 15.54% — 而且只因為透過 MG No.1 Pty Ltd 持有的 45,447 股 B 類 Super Common。三位創辦人的持股載體（MG No.1、Strat Cap No.1、Inbocalupo No.1）各持 B 類的 33.33%，全體主管與董事合計以 12.99% 的 A 類股掌握 45.94% 的投票權。請說「掌握 15.5% 投票權」，不要說「持股逾 10%」 Schedule 13D
7 本公司於 2025-12-19 透過與 Roth CH Acquisition Co. 的 SPAC 合併上市 PARTLY-CONFIRMED — 兩處日期錯誤與一處實質錯誤 業務合併完成日是 2025-12-17，不是 2025-12-19：「On December 17, 2025, the Company completed its previously announced business combination pursuant to the Business Combination Agreement dated January 28, 2025.」Roth 由開曼群島遷冊至德拉瓦州，方式是併入 Roth CH Holdings Inc.，隨後更名為 SharonAI Holdings Inc.；SharonAI Inc. 再與其子公司合併並存續為全資子公司。關鍵在於，該次交割並未使股票登上 Nasdaq。 424B4 載明合併後 A 類股與公開認股權證是在「OTC Markets Group, Inc. - Pink Open Market」交易。Nasdaq 掛牌來自另一次包銷發行： 4,166,666 股、每股 $30.00（約 1.25 億美元），Lucid Capital Markets LLC 擔任承銷團代表，Form 8-A12B 與 Nasdaq 認證均於 2026-02-17 申報，公開說明書為 2026-02-19。同一份說明書記載 2026-02-17 的 OTC 價格為 $1.90，對照發行價 $30.00 Form 424B4
8 本公司募得 9 億美元 CONFIRMED — 但這個講法把募資規模低估了 44% 10-Q：「the Company received aggregate gross proceeds of approximately $900 million before deducting placement agent fees and other offering costs」，來自私募 6,719,896 股 A 類股，每股 $68.73，以及 6,374,823 份預先出資認股權證，每份 $68.2799、行使價 $0.0001；該次發行於 2026-06-22 交割。這 9 億美元是 2026-06-18 宣布、2026-06-29 完成之 16 億美元整體方案的股權腿，另一腿是 7 億美元、4.75% 到期日 2032 年的可轉換優先公司債。公司具名之基石投資人：Situational Awareness L.P. 與 Oaktree Capital Management, L.P. 管理之基金。Goldman Sachs & Co. LLC 為主要配售代理，Lucid Capital Markets 為共同配售代理，Macquarie Capital 為財務顧問，Sheppard Mullin Richter & Hampton LLP 為法律顧問 Form 10-Q
9 可轉換公司債只有 4.75% 這一檔 PARTLY-CONFIRMED — 實際上有兩檔 第一檔：「On April 26, 2026, the Company entered into a Securities Purchase Agreement with certain qualified institutional buyers for the private placement of $350.0 million aggregate principal amount of 6.00% Convertible Senior Notes due 2031」，2026-05-20 交割。第二檔：「On June 22, 2026, the Company issued $700 million aggregate principal amount of 4.75% Convertible Senior Notes due June 15, 2032」，初始轉換價約 $99.66。兩份信託契約皆獨立禁止未經 Required Holder 同意發放現金股利。另有一處疑點： Oaktree 於 2026-08-10 申報之 Schedule 13G/A 稱「$375.0 million of 6% Convertible Senior Notes due in 2031」，與 10-Q 的 3.50 億美元發行規模相差 2,500 萬美元，兩份文件表面上都沒有解釋，引用任一數字前應先釐清 Form 10-Q · Schedule 13G/A
10 與 NVIDIA 的合作屬實、為期六年、金額約 40 億美元 CONFIRMED — 由兩個獨立面向佐證 公司 2026-06-12 新聞稿：與 NVIDIA 的六年期 AI 基礎設施運算合作，在澳洲啟用 72MW 新增機房量能，採用 NVIDIA 的 DSX AI factory 設計，最多部署 40,000 顆 Grace Blackwell GB300 GPU，並以最高 US$4.88 billion 的 Master Cloud Services Agreement 為基礎，結構上是收益分潤與信用支持模式：「Sharon AI will sell NVIDIA-powered cloud services, and NVIDIA will earn both standard product revenue and a share of the cloud revenue on the supported capacity.」James Manning 具名受訪。10-Q 佐證該安排與其約 40 億美元規模，並確認募資用途。MCSA 上限請用 48.8 億美元，會計數字請用約 40 億美元 — 兩者是不同的量度，不是矛盾 NVIDIA 合作新聞稿
11 GPU 部署目標為 2027 年中逾 62,000 顆 NVIDIA GPU CONFIRMED — 但必須附上基準日 10-Q：「the Company had total AI Factory capacity of approximately 132MW, of which 116MW had been contracted to end customers, with deployment of more than 62,000 NVIDIA GPUs expected by mid-2027.」注意這個數字會動：2026-06-12 的 NVIDIA 新聞稿寫的是 2027 年中「more than 55,000 total NVIDIA GPUs」，對應 132MW 總量能與 102MW 已簽約。以各自日期而言兩者皆正確；已簽約量能由 102MW 升至 116MW，GPU 目標隨之上調。引用 62,000 時請附上 2026-06-30 的基準日 Form 10-Q

The 15 MW arrangement has an initial term of 120 months from the Ready-for-Service date (targeted September 26, 2026), with monthly recurring fees based on contracted kilowatt capacity.」出租方一側亦獨立佐證：GreenSquareDC 位於 Norwest 的 SYD1 園區已取得第一期開發許可，釋出初期 15MW 的 AI 就緒量能，目標 2026 年 Q3 完工，全園區約 110MW，總承包商為 Erilyan。15MW 與 2026 年 Q3 兩個數字由雙方對得起來 Form 10-Q

As of June 30, 2026, no liability has been recognized because services have not commenced.」營運面亦由目前開放中的 Network Engineer — Auckland, New Zealand 職缺（回報對象為 Head of Operations）佐證。可陳述範圍有兩道硬性限制： 紐西蘭機房營運商與終端客戶在公開紀錄中皆未具名，且因服務尚未開始而未認列任何負債 Form 10-Q · 徵才頁
14 US$373 百萬合約項下的 2,048 顆 NVIDIA B300 部署在紐西蘭基礎設施上（前一版） CONTRADICTED（已被反證） 公司自家 2026-08-04 針對該合約的新聞稿，把初期 2,048 顆 NVIDIA Blackwell Ultra B300 的部署放在 澳洲、於 Sharon AI 自有 AI 基礎設施上 — 不是紐西蘭。兩份不同的合約被混為一談： 2026-07-16 宣布、與未具名全球 AI lab 簽訂的 US$13.2 億五年雲端運算服務合約，那一份才與紐西蘭量能相關；以及 2026-08-04 宣布、與一家全球 AI 平台簽訂的 US$3.73 億五年 AI 雲端服務合約。公司在第二份新聞稿中明確表述其為獨立於前者的新合約。不得合併這兩份合約的金額或地點 US$373m 新聞稿
15 德拉瓦州 UCC 查詢路徑壞掉（HTTP 404），意味著重試也許會成功 CONTRADICTED — 404 是症狀，不是原因 重跑該入口永遠不會成功。德拉瓦州不提供任何免費的線上公開 UCC 債務人查詢。corp.delaware.gov/uccsearch/ 載明：「Effective December 1, 2001, all non 'Search to Reflect' UCC Searches will be performed by a Delaware Authorized Searcher」——即由官方公布清單中的付費商業中介執行。本次共測試五種 URL 變體，全部回 HTTP 404，因為該端點對公眾根本不存在。正確的下一步是委託 Delaware Authorized Searcher 針對五家德拉瓦州債務人（SharonAI Holdings Inc.、SharonAI Inc.、SharonAI Operations LLC、SharonAI Hosting LLC、SAI US No. 1 LLC）查詢，而不是重試網站。前一版的警語仍然成立且應重申：本次未讀到任何一筆 UCC 歸檔，這件事絕不可在任何地方登錄成「查無留置權」 德拉瓦州 UCC 查詢公告

21 除了一個德州 Austin 的會計職缺之外，美國沒有任何營運據點 PARTLY-CONFIRMED — 大致成立，但有兩點補充 已確認： 現行徵才頁共十五個開放職缺，每一個都在雪梨、墨爾本、澳洲全境、奧克蘭或新加坡 — 沒有一個在美國。唯一的美國職缺證據仍是「Sharon AI, Inc hiring Technical Accountant in Austin, TX」這則 LinkedIn 貼文，而 LinkedIn 公司頁把總部列為雪梨、紐約為次要辦公室。補充一： 公司現在有一位職稱明確涵蓋美國的主管，Wolf Schubert 擔任 Country Manager – USA，其前任聘僱契約是透過德拉瓦州子公司 SharonAI Operations LLC 簽訂 — 因此存在美國僱用主體，不只是通訊地址。補充二： ARIN 號碼資源掛在一個美國組織紀錄之下。正確描述：美國有一層薄但真實的公司與登記存在，沒有美國營運足跡，路由表上也沒有美國網路 徵才頁

4. 領導層與所有權

本節證據等級：primary-record（一手紀錄）＝SEC 申報、網路號碼登錄機構、法院案卷、競選財務申報，或公司自家已發布頁面｜corroborated（多方佐證）＝兩個以上獨立來源互相印證｜single-source（單一來源）＝僅一個來源、無佐證｜GAP＝已具名搜尋但查無。

進入表格前有三項前提。第一，本公司公布了異常完整的具名名冊 — 2026-07-13 申報的 DEF 14A 附有完整的主管與董事名表及個人簡歷，sharonai.com/about/ 另有二十一人的管理層目錄與個別介紹頁。姓名在此案不是問題，可觸及性才是：全公司只有一個員工個人 email 被公布 — Ross.barrows@sharonai.com — 而公司自己公布了十九個 LinkedIn URL。第二，登記幹部與網路聯絡人以獨立列呈現並明確標示，且此處有一項重要的反面發現：在本案中，網路登記機構產不出任何一位真人 — ARIN 與 APNIC 的每一個窗口都是角色帳號，PeeringDB 的 poc_set 是空的。這是實質發現，不是研究失敗。第三，關於一條假聯絡管道的嚴正警告：十九個管理層個人頁面每一頁都顯示 123example@gmail.com 與 @123example。 那是未替換的網站樣板佔位字串，所有頁面（含執行長頁）完全相同。那不是聯絡管道，絕不可登錄為聯絡管道。

線上無法取得 — 德拉瓦州 UCC-1 擔保權人與債務人簽署人 UCC 融資聲明當事人 本次已確立根本原因： corp.delaware.gov/uccsearch/ 載明「Effective December 1, 2001, all non 'Search to Reflect' UCC Searches will be performed by a Delaware Authorized Searcher」。因此德拉瓦州並不提供任何免費的線上公開 UCC 債務人查詢 — 那是付費中介服務。本次測試五種候選 URL；icis.corp.delaware.gov/Ecorp/UccSearch/UccSearchEntity.aspx 與四種變體全部回 HTTP 404。這是結構性阻擋，不是壞掉的連結，再怎麼重試入口網站都不會解決 2026-08-11 嘗試 corp.delaware.gov/uccsearch

4.3 採購決策圈

與創辦人自有的私人業者不同，本公司具備完整董事會、稽核與風險委員會、剛上任的法務長，以及正在進行中的財務長交接。這裡確實存在採購層級，而且正在換手。接觸順序在本案的重要性高於本系列任何其他案子 — 而自上一版以來最重要的變化是：現在有一位具名、職掌涵蓋美國的主管可以先打電話。

Ross Barrows — Head of Capital Strategy & Investor Relations 不是買家，但他是唯一一位有公開個人 email 的 Sharon AI 員工，因此也是全案冷接觸產出最高的入口。 他另有 對 NEXTDC 連續 13 年的股票研究覆蓋，NEXTDC 正是公司主要的機房夥伴，因此他對基礎設施經濟的理解遠超一般 IR 把他當作轉介窗口，不是推銷對象。 Ross.barrows@sharonai.com 是公司公布的真實地址。一封簡短、具體、不帶推銷味、請他指出正確技術負責人的信，成功率遠高於通用表單投遞 — 而徵才頁與聯絡頁的表單是僅有的另外兩條 inbound 管道
未具名但已證實存在 — Chief Information Security Officer；Head of Operations；Head of Governance, Risk & Compliance；Infrastructure Engineering Team Lead 這四個職位由現行職缺中載明的回報線證實存在 —「Head of Governance, Risk & Compliance（澳洲）：reports to CISO」、「Network Engineer（紐西蘭奧克蘭）：reports to Head of Operations」、「Workplace Technician（雪梨）：reports to Head of Operations」、「Senior Data & Storage Engineer（AI Neocloud）：reports to Infrastructure Engineering Team Lead」。CISO 與 Head of Operations 直接位於任何基礎設施採購的路徑上，而兩者在 SEC 申報、管理層目錄與網路登記機構中都沒有具名 把在任者列為下一輪的具名目標。 在那之前，進入營運層的實務路徑是 Data Centre Operations Manager（雪梨）職缺與 Head of Operations 回報線 — 也就是請 Schubert 或 Barrows 告訴你這兩個位置由誰擔任。不要猜名字

4.4 未填補的職位 — 每一項都是 GAP

GAP — Chief Information Security Officer。 由開放中的「Head of Governance, Risk & Compliance（澳洲）」職缺載明「reports to CISO」證實存在。SEC 申報、管理層目錄、新聞稿與網路登記機構中皆無姓名。高優先：CISO 直接位於基礎設施採購的核准路徑上。 · GAP — Head of Operations。 由兩則向其回報的開放職缺證實存在：「Network Engineer（紐西蘭奧克蘭）」與「Workplace Technician／End-User Services（雪梨）」。各處皆未具名。這是技術長在營運面的對應者，也是本案第二有價值的缺名。 · GAP — Infrastructure Engineering Team Lead。 由開放中的「Senior Data & Storage Engineer（AI Neocloud）」職缺回報線證實存在。未具名。 · GAP — Head of Governance, Risk & Compliance。 目前為開放職缺，回報對象為 CISO，亦即該位置是空缺，不只是未具名。 · GAP — Data Centre Operations Manager（雪梨）。 目前為開放職缺。空缺。 · GAP — Head of PMO／PMO Lead（澳洲）。 目前為開放職缺，回報對象為「senior leadership」。空缺。 · GAP — Network Administrator（澳洲）與 Network Engineer（紐西蘭奧克蘭）。 兩者目前皆為開放職缺。這很可能正是每一個 ARIN 與 APNIC 窗口都是角色帳號的原因：很可能根本沒有常設的具名網路人員可以登記。 · GAP — 採購副總、採購經理或供應商管理職。 DEF 14A 主管名表、S-1、21 人管理層目錄與十五個開放職缺中，皆無此類職稱。以約 70 人管理約 40 億美元採購承諾來看，採購權責看來是落在執行長、營運長、技術長與財務長個人身上。再次確認為真實的結構性缺位，不是研究疏漏。 · GAP — 任何形式的具名 NOC 或網路工程人員。 ARIN（兩個 POC 皆 isRoleAccount=Y）、APNIC（三個 handle 皆為 vCard kind=group）、PeeringDB（poc_set 為空）與 bgp.he.net 上都是零位真人。這是實質的反面發現，而且現在是在兩個登記機構、而非一個上獲得確認。 · GAP — Anuj Goel 的 LinkedIn URL。 他是有 Forms 3 與 4 在案的 Section 16 主管，並將於 2026-08-24 出任財務長，但他在 sharonai.com 管理層目錄中沒有頁面，因此不像其他 19 人有公司公布的 LinkedIn 連結。 · GAP — Tal Cohen 的簡介、確切職稱與 LinkedIn。 顧問委員會表中列為「Advisory Board/Business Development」，但其介紹頁回傳零位元組。他是 21 位具名人士中唯一沒有可用個人檔案者。 · GAP — Leopold Aschenbrenner 與 Carl Shulman 的 LinkedIn URL 及任何個人聯絡管道。 兩人合計申報公司 19.9%，除了 Situational Awareness LP 位於 77 Federal Street Floor 4, San Francisco CA 94107 的登記地址之外，都無從觸及。 · GAP — Zachary Nevas 的 LinkedIn 與其在 IMS Investor Relations 的直撥電話。 僅公布代理機構總機 +1 203.972.9200 與共用信箱 sharonai@imsinvestorrelations.com。 · GAP — 除 Ross.barrows@sharonai.com 以外的每一個個人 email 與直撥電話。 十九個管理層個人頁面中有十八個未公布任何真實聯絡方式，只有 123example@gmail.com 這個樣板佔位字串。 · GAP — 五家德拉瓦州實體的註冊代理人、發起人與年報簽署人。 本次已帶有效 __VIEWSTATE 把名稱查詢跑起來，回傳「An error occurred while processing the request」，原因是兩個 CAPTCHA 控制項。不破解 CAPTCHA。需付費登記服務或人工查閱。 · GAP — 所有德拉瓦州 UCC-1 擔保權人、債務人與簽署人。 線上結構性無法取得： 德拉瓦州自 2001-12-01 起，所有非「Search to Reflect」查詢都必須由 Delaware Authorized Searcher 執行。需委託認證查詢商。 · GAP — 德州州務卿之公司幹部與 UCC 紀錄。 SOSDirect 與 handle DSSL1-AP 浮現 — 是集團中最老的網域，早了二十年，也是最有機會找到遮罩前註冊人紀錄的對象。 · GAP — USPTO 商標資料。 連「SHARON AI」是否為已註冊之美國商標都仍未知，因此無法取得聲明簽署人或通訊代理人。tmsearch.uspto.gov API 之 GET 回 S3 NoSuchKey、POST 回 PA。本公司在美國沒有任何會被估價機關登錄的不動產。

AI」會回 10,367 筆、且以 Clearview AI 為主，必須用片語引號。 · LinkedIn 公司頁 — 員工數級距、總部雪梨與次要紐約辦公室、成立於 2024 年，以及浮現 Deepth Dinesan 待查線索的員工清單。

已觸及但無實質產出者。 sharonai.com/contacts/ — 無街道地址、無電話、無 email、無具名個人；只有 Equinix SYD3／SYD5 與 NEXTDC M3 的據點卡與「Contact Us」表單觸發鈕。 · sharonai.com/management/tal-cohen/ — 回傳零位元組，因此唯一一位沒有取得簡介與 LinkedIn 的顧問委員仍是缺口。 · AS401247 之 PeeringDB API — {"data": [], "meta": {"error": "Entity not found"}}；ARIN 側 ASN 在 PeeringDB 完全沒有紀錄，與其從未被路由過一致。 · sharonai.com、sharonai.ai 與 distributedstorage.com 的 WHOIS — 三者皆有隱私遮罩；僅在註冊商與日期中繼資料上有用，並使 distributedstorage.com（建立於 2002-08-15）浮現為集團中最老的網域。 · 研討會與社群掃描，涵蓋 GTC、SC、NANOG、Cloudfest、DataCenter World、HostingCon 的講者資料，以及 Reddit、Hacker News、LowEndTalk 與 WebHostingTalk — 找不到任何 Sharon AI 講者簡介，也找不到任何已查證的員工帳號。這是有意義的反面發現。

被阻擋者，及原因。 德拉瓦州公司司實體名稱查詢 — 與前一版不同，本次已取得有效的 __VIEWSTATE（596 bytes）、__VIEWSTATEGENERATOR 與 __EVENTVALIDATION，並以帶 session 的完整 POST 查詢「SHARONAI」。伺服器回 HTTP 200、內容為「An error occurred while processing the request」且零筆結果，原因是那兩個 CAPTCHA 控制項。不破解 CAPTCHA。此阻擋現在是已診斷出原因，不只是被觀察到。 · 德拉瓦州 UCC — 結構性阻擋。 測試五種 URL 變體，全部 HTTP 404。根本原因：自 2001-12-01 起，所有非「Search to Reflect」之 UCC 查詢皆須由 Delaware Authorized Searcher 執行。德拉瓦州不存在免費的線上公開 UCC 債務人查詢；重試入口網站是徒勞。 · 德州 — sos.state.tx.us/ucc 與 direct.sos.state.tx.us 需帳號且掛有系統異常公告；mycpa.cpa.state.tx.us/coa/ 會 302 轉址至 JavaScript 渲染頁面，POST 回傳同一份 130,041 bytes 的外框、無任何結果列。屬不確定，不是反面發現。 · opensecrets.org（curl 與 WebFetch 皆回 HTTP 403）· whoisrequest.com（HTTP 403，帶 JS 挑戰）· viewdns.info 的 whois 歷史（404 頁）· whoxy.com（需付費帳號）· trademarks.justia.com（HTTP 403）· trademarkia.com（Cloudflare 挑戰）· tmsearch.uspto.gov API（GET 回 S3 NoSuchKey、POST 回 MethodNotAllowed）· assignment-api.uspto.gov（連線失敗）· api.opencorporates.com（HTTP 401，需 token）· publicreporting.elections.ny.gov（HTTP 403）· projects.propublica.org/itemizer 查詢（404）· fec.report（連線失敗）· forebears.io 在約 25 次查詢後（HTTP 403，冷卻後未恢復）。

補齊其餘缺口的最佳下一步。 (1) 寄信給 Ross.barrows@sharonai.com，那是唯一公布的個人地址，請他轉介至技術負責人。 (2) 致電 IMS Investor Relations 的 Zachary Nevas：+1 203.972.9200，那是唯一附在具名真人身上的美國直撥號碼。 (3) 透過 linkedin.com/in/wolf-schubert-a9b6a5229 接觸 Country Manager – USA 的 Wolf Schubert — 他就是簡報所要找的那位美國簽字人。 (4) 委託 Delaware Authorized Searcher 針對五家德拉瓦州債務人查 UCC 全貌。 (5) 由不同來源位址重跑 forebears.io，補齊共和黨側的姓氏語源。 (6) 對 distributedstorage.com 而非 sharonai.com 重跑歷史 WHOIS，因為它早了二十年，最可能保有遮罩前的紀錄。

5. 據點與機房

一句話總結：完全沒有美國資料中心據點。 所有營運據點都在澳洲或紐西蘭。原本會讓此案成為一組帳戶的德州案場，已於 2026-01-13 出售。

據點 機房營運商 自有或租用 規模／電力（僅列已公布者） 證據
NEXTDC M3，West Footscray，墨爾本，澳洲（Tier IV）— 旗艦，也是唯一確認已上線的叢集 NEXTDC Limited（ASX: NXT） 租用（colocation） — 不可取消之營業租賃安排 承載 「SharonAI Supercluster」——依 NVIDIA reference architecture 建置的 1,016 顆 GPU 部署，目前配置 NVIDIA H200 與 NVIDIA Quantum-2 InfiniBand。官方描述為「immediately available installed capacity」，以 bonded 200G Ethernet 提供 VM、容器或 Bare Metal。GAP：未公布此機房的 MW 數字 S-1：「Central to this partnership is the SharonAI Supercluster, a 1,016-GPU deployment built to NVIDIA reference architecture specifications… This is co-located at NEXTDC's M3 data center in Melbourne, Australia and currently features NVIDIA H200 GPUs.」獨立佐證：PeeringDB netfac 紀錄 85369「NEXTDC M3」，West Footscray, AU
NEXTDC S3，Artarmon，雪梨，澳洲 NEXTDC Limited（ASX: NXT） 租用（colocation） 1,024 顆 NVIDIA B300 GPU 叢集，預計 2026 上半年上線，與 Cisco 合作建置，定位為 「Australia's first Cisco Secure AI Factory」 S-1：「We are also deploying a 1,024-unit NVIDIA B300 GPU cluster which will be located at NEXTDC's S3 data center… This cluster is also built in partnership with Cisco.」佐證：PeeringDB netfac 紀錄 89484「NEXTDC S3」，Artarmon, AU，2026-07-29 建立
NEXTDC — 墨爾本與雪梨擴充量能（2026 年 7 月修訂重簽之服務訂單） NEXTDC Limited（ASX: NXT） 租用／已簽約量能 72 MW；合約總承諾 約 6.23 億美元；分五階段自 2027 年 4 月至 2027 年 8 月部署。另有透過 NEXTDC 取得最高 87MW 的擴充協議 10-Q：「In July 2026, the Company executed an amended and restated service order with NEXTDC for 72 MW of data center capacity in Australia… approximately $623 million over the term… deployed in five phases commencing in April 2027 through August 2027.」風險因子：「we have secured up to 87MW of capacity through NEXTDC and rely on NEXTDC to host substantially all of our GPU infrastructure」
GreenSquareDC — SYD1 園區（亦稱 SYDGPU1），Norwest Business Park，雪梨，澳洲 GreenSquareDC（Partners Group 支持） 租用 — 長期資料中心服務協議 15 MW，2026 年 3 月簽訂；自目標 Ready-for-Service 日 2026-09-26 起算 120 個月初始期間；月費依已簽約 kW 計算。園區設計滿載可達 110MW，支援高密度液冷配置 10-Q；S-1
紐西蘭 — 未揭露之機房 GAP — 未揭露營運商。 託管服務由 Andrew Sjoquist Enterprises（ASE） 承接 租用／已簽約量能 14 MW，用以支援長期客戶服務協議下的雲端運算基礎設施部署，預計 2027 年 Q1 開始。這是撐起 US$13.2 億 AI lab 合約的據點 10-Q：「secured additional data center capacity in New Zealand for 14MW… with commencement expected in Q1 2027.」ASE 承諾約 AUD$400 千元／五年，係於終止 DSS 資料中心服務安排後訂立
其他第三方量能，約 29.6 MW（2026 年 5 月） GAP — 多家未揭露之第三方業者 租用／已簽約 約 29.6 MW，安排預計自 2026 年底開始 10-Q Key Corporate Milestones
Equinix SYD3 與 SYD5，雪梨（Tier III） — 舊有或行銷說法，申報文件未佐證 Equinix 租用（colocation） 未量化。 官網列為資料中心據點，但10-Q 與 S-1 皆未佐證——兩者均稱 NEXTDC 承載「substantially all」GPU 基礎設施。視為舊有或小規模據點 sharonai.com 首頁列出 Sydney: Equinix SYD3 & SYD5（Tier III）。任何 SEC 申報中查無
Texas Critical Data Centers LLC（TCDC）— Ector County／Odessa，德州 原為與 New Era Energy & Digital Inc.（NUAI） 的 50:50 合資；現已全數歸 NUAI 已出售，不再持有 — 2026-01-13 原規劃 250MW 淨零表後資料中心，搭配天然氣發電廠，位於 Permian Basin；2025 年公告時已取得 Odessa 郊外 235 英畝，並就另一塊相鄰 203 英畝簽有 LOI（合計 438 英畝） 10-Q：「On January 13, 2026, the Company completed the sale of its 50% ownership interest in TCDC to NUAI. Upon completion of the transaction, the Company ceased to have an ownership interest in TCDC or participate in the joint venture.」對價包含 NUAI 發行之 5,000 萬美元、10.0% 優先擔保可轉換本票（已於 2026-04-24 以現金全額償還，未轉換）以及 NUAI 普通股（於 2026-05-14 出售）。認列出售利益：截至 2026-06-30 之六個月 $65,920 千美元

已揭露之總量： AI Factory 總量能約 132MW，其中 116MW 已與終端客戶簽約，且截至 2026-06-30 尚有 132MW 已簽約資料中心量能（10-Q）。

集中度備註（可談的話題，不是缺陷指控）： 公司自家的風險因子用語——「we rely on NEXTDC to host substantially all of our GPU infrastructure」——本身就是明示的單一機房依賴，再加上對單一整合商 2.562 億美元的預付款曝險。這一組合，正是值得對稽核與風險管理委員會種下的治理提問；見第 14 節。

6. 硬體機隊

本節證據等級：確認（confirmed）＝SEC 申報或公司自家頁面之第一手具名揭露，或多方獨立佐證｜情況證據（circumstantial）＝行為或名單指向某處，但未有任何採購關係證據｜推論（inferred）＝僅由品牌標記、腳位或機構形式導出｜GAP＝正反皆無資料。

供應商／類別 證據等級 證據實際上說了什麼
World Wide Technology — WWT Australia Pty Ltd 確認 — 既有整合商，也是最大的單一硬體對手方 10-Q Note 10：「prepayments primarily relate to deposits and milestone payments for high-performance computing equipment with WWT Australia Pty Ltd」——截至 2026-06-30 帳上 $256,186 千美元（2.562 億美元） 設備預付款。WWT 亦在 S-1 中被列為構成整合式 AI 生態系的策略夥伴之一。HPC 設備實際上就是透過這條通路採購的
Lenovo — Lenovo Group Limited；Lenovo Global Financial Services (Australia & New Zealand) Pty Limited 確認（商業關係） — 五項獨立佐證 (1) 10-Q Note 10：「down payments under an Equipment-as-a-Service (EaaS)/Device-as-a-Service (DaaS) arrangement with Lenovo Global Financial Services (Australia & New Zealand) Pty Limited」——預付 $33,094 千美元。(2) 10-Q 承諾段：「On December 12, 2025, the Company entered into a Statement of Work with Lenovo Global Financial Services (Australia & New Zealand) Pty Limited for managed infrastructure services… term of 60 months… upfront payment representing approximately 50% of the total contract value.」(3) 10-Q 中出現 XBRL 標籤 SHAZ:LenovoGlobalFinancialServicesLimitedMember。(4) S-1 將 Lenovo Group Limited 列為策略技術夥伴。(5) 公司自家節點規格列有 「24 x 32GB TruDDR5 4800MHz RAM」（sharonai.com/ai-cloud/）。請注意其結構：硬體是以融資服務方式消費，不是直接買斷——這是相當硬的競爭護城河
Lenovo ThinkSystem SR675 V3 為該公開節點的實際平台 推論 — 很強，但仍是推論而非揭露 TruDDR5 是 Lenovo 專有的記憶體品牌名稱，因此其出現在公開規格中，指向 SR675 V3 等級的 Lenovo ThinkSystem 硬體，而非白牌或其他 OEM。該公司在任何地方都未具名機殼或系統型號。 這裡刻意標為推論——Lenovo 的商業關係是確認的，這個特定平台判定不是
Cisco Systems Inc. 確認 — 而且是本案中的直接伺服器競爭對手 S-1 將其列為策略夥伴：「Cisco provides the Company with AI-ready networking infrastructure, including access to Cisco Nexus HyperFabric AI and NVIDIA Spectrum-X-aligned」基礎設施。公司為「a strategic partner of Cisco」且為 Cisco 360 Partner Program 成員，該資格「authorizes us to build, sell, and manage integrated Cisco solutions」。NEXTDC S3 的 B300 叢集為「built in partnership with Cisco and is expected to be Australia's first Cisco Secure AI Factory」。Digital Alpha 新聞稿 明列 Cisco UCS 伺服器、Cisco Nexus Hyperfabric 交換，以及 Cisco 資安與可觀測性堆疊。與 Cisco 具獨家技術合作關係的 Digital Alpha Advisors LLC 投資最高 US$200M 並為股東，形成結構性鎖定
NVIDIA Corporation 確認 — 而且是實質掌控 OEM 短名單的一方 於 2026-06-12 簽訂六年 AI 基礎設施運算合作；約 40 億美元採購承諾；最高 40,000 顆 GB300；採 NVIDIA DSX AI factory design；收益分潤與信用支持模式。公司為認證 NVIDIA Cloud Partner（NCP），可見 NVIDIA 12 至 18 個月滾動供應鏈預測，並對 B200／B300 具優先取得權（10-Q；S-1）
AMD 確認 — 兩種不同關係 CPU： 公開節點規格於 H100 NVL 與 L40S 兩款節點皆使用「2 x AMD EPYC 32C 3.25GHz CPUs」。GPU： AMD MI300X 曾以 $2.90/GPU/Hr 列於公開型錄（sharonai.com/ai-cloud/；Wayback 2024-12-08）
VAST Data Inc.（經 Dicker Data Limited 採購） 確認 10-Q Note 10：「prepayments to Dicker Data Limited for VAST Data software」——$13,368 千美元。2026-06-16 宣布部署 600PB VAST AI Operating System。Dicker Data 為登記在案的澳洲代理商
Megaport Limited 確認 於 S-1 中被列為構成整合式 AI 生態系的策略夥伴（連接／互連）。PeeringDB 佐證：AS142588 於 MegaIX Melbourne 對接，103.26.71.53，10Gbps
NEXTDC Limited（ASX: NXT） 確認 主要、非獨家之 colocation 業者。「we rely on NEXTDC to host substantially all of our GPU infrastructure」、「we have secured up to 87MW of capacity through NEXTDC」。不是伺服器供應商，但是機房守門人（10-Q）
Andrew Sjoquist Enterprises（ASE） 確認 託管服務商，依不可取消承諾約 AUD$400 千元／五年，係於終止 Distributed Storage Solutions（DSS）資料中心服務安排時訂立；支援紐西蘭部署（10-Q）
Super Micro Computer, Inc. 情況證據 — 不得誇大。絕不可陳述為採購關係。 Supermicro 在整份 10-Q、DEF 14A、三份 8-K 與 4.6 MB S-1 中只出現一次，而且不是採購關係。那唯一一次出現在 NVIDIA Cloud Partner 身分所帶來的利益清單裡：「OEM Relationships: Facilitated engagement with OEMs such as Cisco, Lenovo, Dell, and Super Micro to support server procurement and integration」（S-1）。那是 NVIDIA 促成對一組 OEM 名單的接觸。它只能證明 Supermicro 在已認可、已知的 OEM 名單內，除此之外什麼都不能證明。沒有採購單、沒有預付款、沒有承諾、沒有具名合約，任何公開節點規格中也沒有 Supermicro 硬體。 所有公開節點規格都指向 Lenovo。等級為情況證據，不是確認
Dell Technologies 情況證據 與 Supermicro 完全同一句 S-1：「OEM Relationships: Facilitated engagement with OEMs such as Cisco, Lenovo, Dell, and Super Micro」。全網無任何採購證據。 僅因 NVIDIA NCP 身分而被列為已知 OEM 通路（S-1）
Hewlett Packard Enterprise（HPE）、Gigabyte、ASUS、Penguin Computing、Pure Storage、WEKA、Arista GAP 已針對 10-Q、DEF 14A、S-1、徵才頁與產品頁明確檢索。上述任一供應商皆為零次出現。 正反皆無證據——記錄為真正的「不存在資料」，而非反面發現

公開節點規格 — 目前最接近 BOM 層級的採購樣貌

以下逐字讀自公司自家現行 /ai-cloud/ 頁面。這是本檔中最有用的硬體物證，因為這是唯一一處公司描述「一台機器」而非「一座叢集」的地方。

節點 公開規格（依原結構） Supermicro 對應機種
H100 NVL 運算節點 8 × H100 NVL（94GB） 搭 NVLink Bridge（188GB）；2 × AMD EPYC 32C 3.25GHz CPU（頁面顯示為「3954」，幾乎可以確定是 EPYC 9354——屬推論，已標記）；24 × 32GB TruDDR5 4800MHz RAM（合計 768GB）；6 × 7.68TB U.2 NVMe（約 46TB）；2 × 1.92TB M.2 NVMe（3.84TB 系統碟） AS-4125GS-TNRT（4U、8 張 PCIe 雙寬 GPU、雙路 AMD EPYC 9004/9005、24 條 DIMM 插槽、前置 NVMe）——在 GPU 數量、腳位型式、DIMM 數與碟位拓撲上是真正的同級對比
L40S 運算節點 8 × L40S（48GB）；2 × AMD EPYC 32C 3.25GHz CPU；24 × 32GB TruDDR5 4800MHz RAM（768GB）；6 × 7.68TB U.2 NVMe（約 46TB）；2 × 1.92TB M.2 NVMe（3.84TB 系統碟） SYS-421GE-TNRT，或同一款 AS-4125GS-TNRT 機殼搭 8 張 L40S PCIe
SharonAI Supercluster — 已安裝、運轉中 1,016 顆 GPU，目前為 NVIDIA H200、NVIDIA Quantum-2 InfiniBand，依 NVIDIA reference architecture 建置，位於 NEXTDC M3 墨爾本。叢集其餘部分預期為 NVIDIA B200（S-1） H200 SXM 8-GPU：SYS-821GE-TNHR／AS-8125GS-TNHR（8U／4U HGX H200 8-GPU）。若為 PCIe H200 NVL：AS-4125GS-TNRT 等級
NEXTDC S3 的 B300 叢集 — 部署中 1,024 顆 NVIDIA B300（Blackwell Ultra），預計 2026 上半年上線，與 Cisco 合作，定位為「Australia's first Cisco Secure AI Factory」（S-1） Supermicro HGX B300 8-GPU 系統（SYS-A21GE-NBRT 等級／4U 液冷 Blackwell Ultra）。注意：這座叢集的具名夥伴是 Cisco——這是 Cisco UCS 的替換標的，不是全新場域
NVIDIA 六年合作 — 最大的未來機隊事件 在澳洲建置 72MW AI factory，採用 NVIDIA DSX AI factory design，規模最高至 40,000 顆 Grace Blackwell GB300。 以收益分潤與信用支持架構。協議日期 2026-06-12。相應之額外 HPC 硬體與相關基礎設施採購承諾 約 40 億美元（10-Q） GB300 NVL72 機櫃級。 注意：NVIDIA DSX 參考設計加上收益分潤與信用支持結構，代表 NVIDIA 對 OEM 短名單具強大影響力——這不是純粹的公開競標
儲存架構 將於 AI 雲端基礎設施上部署 600PB 的 VAST AI Operating System（2026-06-16 宣布），公司自訂基準為 每 1,000 顆 GPU 約 6PB，即以約 100,000 顆 GPU 規模設計（10-Q） VAST 通常架在通用 x86 儲存節點上——可能的鄰接機會，但注意 VAST 軟體是透過 Dicker Data Limited 購買，預付 $13,368 千美元
網路態勢 NEXTDC M3 既有量能採 bonded 200G Ethernet；Supercluster 採 NVIDIA Quantum-2 InfiniBand；透過 Cisco 合作導入 Cisco Nexus HyperFabric AI 與 NVIDIA Spectrum-X 對齊之網路 網路面已由 Cisco 與 NVIDIA 牢牢佔住——不是可切入的通道
歷史／既有型錄 SKU NVIDIA H200、H100 NVL、L40S、L40、A40、RTX3090，以及 AMD MI300X（sharonai.com/ai-cloud/） A40／L40／L40S／RTX3090 屬舊世代密度——很可能是最老的自有資產，也是最先的汰換候選

已承諾支出，以及對切入點的意義

為支援已簽約 AI 運算量能，對硬體與基礎設施供應商之採購承諾 約 7.651 億美元 10-Q 承諾段
新五年雲端合約下 2,048 顆 NVIDIA B300 部署之額外承諾（含估計稅負） 約 2.172 億美元 10-Q 期後事項
NVIDIA 六年合作 — 額外 HPC 硬體與相關基礎設施 約 40 億美元 10-Q
已在 WWT Australia Pty Ltd 的設備預付款 $256,186 千美元 10-Q Note 10
已在 Lenovo GFS (ANZ) Pty Limited 的 EaaS／DaaS 頭期款 $33,094 千美元 10-Q Note 10
為 VAST Data 軟體付給 Dicker Data Limited 的預付款 $13,368 千美元 10-Q Note 10

解讀： 7.651 億與 2.172 億美元是已被預定掉的錢。任何動作都必須瞄準這些之外的批次，或是設法重切這些批次。目前約 2,000 顆 GPU 已安裝，對照的是 2027 年年中前超過 62,000 顆的計畫——機隊絕大部分還在前方，不在後方，這是本案結構上唯一真正令人鼓舞的事實。

7. GPU 型錄與 AI 佈局

7.1 最重大的變化：公開費率表已經不存在

截至 2026-08-11，公開費率表已不存在。 sharonai.com/cloud-pricing/ 現在回傳 零位元組；/pricing-calculator/ 301 轉往首頁。自 2025-05-28 起的存檔快照皆不含 GPU 價格文字。 商業模式已轉為「Contact us for volume and term discount」、take-or-pay 合約，以及超大型議價合約（$3.73 億、$9.5 億、$13.2 億）。以下每一個價格都是歷史值——2024 年 10 月與 12 月——不得當作現行價格陳述。

7.2 完整擷取型錄 — 每一個 SKU 與其價格

快照 A — Wayback 2024-12-08，公司公布過的最後一份完整費率表：

7.3 AI 佈局

AI 佈局就是這家公司本身——這不是一家附掛 GPU 產品線的通用主機商。S-1 描述其為「enterprise, government and research organizations」提供「sovereign, low-latency access」的加速運算硬體，目標客群為「AI startups, enterprises, and university researchers」以及「research, government, and enterprise customers requiring GPU resources for LLM training, fine-tuning, and real-time inference」。文件並引述 NEXTDC 具備澳洲聯邦政府 Digital Transformation Agency（DTA）「Certified Strategic」 資格，可承接主權政府工作負載，且 NVIDIA 的「consumption desk」會將合格客戶轉介給本公司。

由證據讀出的世代階梯： A40／L40／L40S／RTX3090（舊世代）→ H100 NVL（約 2024 年 9 月）→ H200（約 2024 年 12 月，即目前已安裝的 1,016 顆 GPU Supercluster）→ B200（Supercluster 其餘部分，2026 上半年）→ B300（NEXTDC S3 的 1,024 顆，2026 上半年；另 2,048 顆自 2027 年 Q1）→ GB300，最高 40,000 顆，2027 年 4 至 8 月。這裡沒有世代缺口——與老牌主機商不同，本業者幾乎即時跟上 NVIDIA 的節奏，這與 NCP 身分及其宣稱可見 NVIDIA 12 至 18 個月滾動供應預測相符。機會不在汰換缺口，而在前方建置量體的規模本身。

8. 採購時鐘

本公司實際承諾新矽晶的頻率，由三組互相獨立、且皆不依賴供應商說法的序列重建而成。

8.1 時間軸
